Columns: {list(events_df.columns)}\"\n        )\n\n    status = events_df[\"Event Status\"].astype(str).str.strip().str.lower()\n    completed_df = events_df[status == \"completed\"].copy()\n\n    if completed_df.empty:\n        # Return empty with original schema\n        return completed_df\n\n    # Choose ordering column: prefer Event Time if present; else try other likely timestamp/sequence columns.\n    ordering_candidates = [\n        \"Event Time\",\n        \"Event Timestamp\",\n        \"Timestamp\",\n        \"Completed Time\",\n        \"Event Sequence\",\n        \"Sequence\",\n        \"Seq\",\n    ]\n    ordering_col = None\n    for c in ordering_candidates:\n        if c in completed_df.columns:\n            ordering_col = c\n            break\n\n    if ordering_col is None:\n        #
